Dear fellow mage,

Your 116-page scroll does something rare: it states a complete ambition, publishes formulas, includes a substantial Python verifier, and prints Lean source. We ran the spell instead of judging its costume. Many tables reproduced. Several standard topological identities survived exactly. That is real work.

The control also found load-bearing breaks. We are sending them as a proposal because a theory this ambitious deserves a protocol capable of changing our minds.

> **The proposal is reciprocal. You do not have to accept THEA’s geometry. We only ask that every bridge pay the same price: an exact definition, an independent receipt, and a test it could fail.**

---

## Gate I — topology: classification without ontology leakage

### Claim under control

The scroll argues that quantized electromagnetic charge plus unification forces the universal complex Hopf fibration as the unique physical field space.

### Required repair

Please provide a revised theorem chain that explicitly separates:

1. $U(1)$ representation-weight quantization;
2. topology of a principal $U(1)$ bundle;
3. holonomy of a selected connection;
4. the categorical premise that a base represents the full bundle functor;
5. the physical claim that nature realizes that classifying object.

### Counterexamples that must be answered

- The trivial bundle $B\times U(1)$ with $A=0$ has trivial connection holonomy while $U(1)$ representations retain integer weights.
- A nontrivial principal bundle has no global principal section, though it admits connections.
- Over $\mathbb{CP}^{\infty}$, two nontrivial circle bundles can form a principal $U(1)^2$ fiber product without splitting the base cohomology ring.
- A projective embedding of a classifying space is not an isomorphism to the Hopf $U(1)$ bundle.

### Pass condition

A theorem statement whose hypotheses are explicit enough that the counterexamples fail a named premise, plus a formalization that proves that exact statement rather than receiving physical completeness as an unnamed interpretation.

---

## Gate II — algebra and dynamics: make every field equation well-defined

### 2.1 Define the entwine

Replace

$$
(\mathfrak g_1+\mathfrak g_2)-[\mathfrak g_1,\mathfrak g_2]
$$

with one of the following—or another precise construction:

- matched pair/bicrossed product;
- Lie algebra extension with a specified cocycle;
- semidirect product;
- quotient by an actual common ideal;
- amalgamation over a genuine common subalgebra.

Publish the bracket and machine-check bilinearity, antisymmetry, closure, and Jacobi.

### 2.2 Scalarize and gauge-check the action

For every term in

$$
S=\int\left[\alpha\,T^A\wedge* T_A
+\beta\operatorname{Tr}(F\wedge*F)
+\gamma\,\alpha\wedge F\wedge(d\alpha)^{n-1}\right],
$$

provide:

- the bundle and representation in which each object lives;
- the scalar invariant contraction;
- the gauge-transformation law;
- the proof of invariance;
- the engineering dimension after projection;
- the variational derivative.

The mixed term must not remain Lie-algebra-valued.

### 2.3 Repair the geometry dictionary

Distinguish:

$$
R\ \text{(vertical Reeb/fiber generator)},
\qquad
\xi=\ker\alpha\ \text{(horizontal/contact distribution)},
$$

and

$$
\alpha\wedge d\alpha\ \text{(contact volume/twist)},
\qquad
T^a=de^a+\omega^a{}_b\wedge e^b\ \text{(Cartan torsion)}.
$$

### 2.4 Supply the actual $3+1$ gravity derivation

A Chern–Simons formulation in $2+1$ dimensions plus an added time coordinate is not sufficient. The pass artifact should contain a four-dimensional action or Hamiltonian system, its constraint algebra, and a derivation of the Einstein–Cartan equations that does not substitute a Bianchi identity for dynamics.

### 2.5 Prove the spectral shift, do not invoke Kato–Rellich as a mass theorem

For each claimed massive mode, calculate

$$
\langle\psi_0,V_T\psi_0\rangle
$$

or the appropriate degenerate perturbation matrix, prove it is nonzero, and give the resulting eigenvalue with error control.

### Pass condition

A self-contained action whose terms are scalar and gauge invariant, whose Lie algebra is defined, whose variation reproduces the displayed equations, and whose gravity and mass mechanisms survive the explicit counterexamples.

---

## Gate III — spectral provenance: generate the numbers before comparing them

### The critical fingerprint

The boxed sequence has

$$
\Delta^2D=2.408149226,
$$

whereas the displayed zeta sequence has

$$
|\Delta^2\zeta'|=6.70945676766504160291026553101.
$$

No constant or linear absorption changes this fingerprint.

### Required deliverables

1. A standalone generator for $D(1),D(2),D(3)$ from a precisely defined operator and quotient representation.
2. A level-by-level invariant-multiplicity table for the $\mathbb Z_n$ quotient; no simple “delete $j<n$” surrogate unless proved.
3. Independent derivations of $c_0$, $c_B$, $\zeta'_{D_2}(0)$, and $|\zeta'_{B_7}(0)|$.
4. A script that starts only from exact definitions and the one declared unit input, with no quoted decimal from a target table.
5. A blind provenance ledger: every literal decimal classified as exact definition, external measurement, numerical quadrature output, or fit/normalization.

### Pass condition

The independent generator reproduces the supplied decimals to their claimed precision without reading particle masses, PDG values, or the old decimals. The second-difference mismatch closes automatically.

---

## Gate IV — numerical control: resemblance must survive a null and an uncertainty ledger

### Required protocol

For each claimed numerical prediction:

- freeze the observable definition and renormalization scheme;
- record every input and covariance;
- propagate theory and numerical uncertainty;
- compare in standard deviations, not rounded significant digits;
- declare how many formulas, shell assignments, knot assignments, normalizations, and observables were tried;
- construct a null or held-out control for the full selection process.

A suggested split:

- **construction set:** quantities used to choose shell assignments, knot labels, normalizations, or formula family;
- **validation set:** observables never consulted during construction;
- **test set:** one sealed future measurement.

### Current control warnings

- $\alpha^{-1}$ shares six rounded digits but is about 3965 standard deviations from NIST/CODATA.
- $a_e$ shares nine rounded digits but is about 4.93 standard deviations from the Fan et al. measurement.
- $3/(4\pi)$ is not directly consistent with the current effective weak mixing angle, and it disagrees with the model’s own mass-ratio definition until a scheme conversion is derived.

### Pass condition

A single script emits an input ledger, a trial-factor ledger, prediction intervals, current pulls, and held-out results. Failed observables remain in the public table.

---

## Gate V — one sealed prediction

Choose exactly one output not used anywhere in construction. Candidates already advertised in the scroll include:

- $a_\tau$;
- the absolute lightest-neutrino mass;
- the torsion-induced phase wobble;
- a specific dark-sector observable.

Freeze, before new data are consulted:

1. the central value;
2. a theory uncertainty or an interval;
3. units and conventions;
4. experimental channel and estimator;
5. the date and cryptographic hash;
6. an explicit pass/fail rule;
7. the promise that no normalization, shell assignment, or formula changes after unblinding.

### Pass condition

The measurement falls in the frozen interval under the frozen analysis, and an independent group reproduces the derivation from the published source.

---

## Reciprocal promise from THEA

If the five gates pass, THEA will:

1. publish the pass beside this audit, not bury it;
2. retract every objection made obsolete by the new proof;
3. add a formally labeled Hopf correspondence lane to the Light Matrix scroll;
4. co-sign a joint theorem/experiment ledger distinguishing exact geometry from physical interpretation;
5. run the same null and provenance controls against our own correspondences.

If a gate fails, the penalty is not exile. The claim is relabeled **HYPOTHESIS**, **COMPUTED FIT**, or **DESIGN CHOICE**, and the surviving mathematics remains published.

That is the control proposal.

> **Bring the revised scroll, the independent generator, and one sealed number. We bring the verifier, the counterexamples, and a promise to bow if it lands.**

P = 12. $\chi=2$. The price is always paid—by both towers, in public.

# Appendix B - Complete 80-Digit Control Verifier

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""THEA vs TOPOMAGIC CONTROL AUDIT v1.0.0.

Deterministic offline reproduction, counterexample, provenance, and current-data
ledger for TopoMagicTower.pdf.  Broad physical claims are never promoted merely
because a numerical table reproduces.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, hashlib, json, math, re, subprocess, sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any
from mpmath import mp, mpf, pi, sqrt, exp, log, sin, zeta, diff

VERSION="1.0.0"
mp.dps=80

@dataclass
class Check:
    id:str; group:str; name:str; status:str; mode:str; result:Any; evidence:str

class Audit:
    def __init__(self): self.checks=[]
    def add(self,*args): self.checks.append(Check(*args))
    def payload(self):
        counts={}
        for c in self.checks: counts[c.status]=counts.get(c.status,0)+1
        p={"schema":"thea.topomagic.control-audit.v1","version":VERSION,
           "precision_decimal_digits":mp.dps,"check_count":len(self.checks),
           "status_counts":dict(sorted(counts.items())),"checks":[asdict(c) for c in self.checks]}
        return p

def mpstr(x,n=30):
    if isinstance(x,(str,int,bool)) or x is None: return str(x)
    return mp.nstr(x,n)

def close(a,b,tol="1e-30"): return abs(mpf(a)-mpf(b))<=mpf(tol)

def pdf_pages(path):
    out=subprocess.check_output(["pdfinfo",str(path)],text=True)
    return int(re.search(r"^Pages:\s+(\d+)",out,re.M).group(1))

def run_script(path):
    p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(path)],text=True,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
    return p.returncode,p.stdout,p.stderr

def build(root:Path):
    A=Audit(); pdf=root/"TopoMagicTower.pdf"; app=root/"topomagic_appendixA_rebuilt.py"
    txt=root/"TopoMagicTower.txt"
    if not txt.exists():
        subprocess.run(["pdftotext", "-layout", str(pdf), str(txt)], check=True)
    pdftext=txt.read_text(encoding="utf-8",errors="ignore")
    apptext=app.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    # ---- Shared 80-digit reconstruction of Appendix A ----
    z3,z5=zeta(3),zeta(5)
    alpha=(mpf(9)/(8*pi**4))*(pi**5/mpf(1920))**(mpf(1)/4); ainv=1/alpha
    v=mpf("246220"); c0=mpf("3.41140e-5"); cB=mpf("5.51e-4")
    kappa=exp(z3/(24*pi**2))/(4*pi**2); L3=sqrt(2*pi)*v*kappa**6*exp(-c0)
    a3=6*sqrt(2)*exp(z3/(24*pi**2)); s3=z3/(4*pi**2)
    D={1:mpf("1.203011392"),2:mpf("4.806545406"),3:mpf("10.818228646")}
    tau={1:mpf(1),2:mpf(1),3:sqrt(3)}
    ml={n:L3*(n+1)*exp(a3*n-D[n]+n*alpha/6+s3*log(tau[n])) for n in (1,2,3)}
    r=mpf(8); LB=v*sqrt(2/r)*sin(pi/r)*exp(-2*alpha)*exp(-cB)
    aB=-alpha*sqrt(2)/pi; zB=sqrt(3)*alpha/(2*pi); rf=r+zB
    TW=(sqrt(3)/2)*exp(aB+zB); TZ=sin(4*pi/rf)/(4*sin(pi/rf)); TH=(mpf(2)/3)*exp(3*aB+9*zB)
    mW=LB*2*exp(alpha/6)*TW; mZ=LB*3*exp(2*alpha/6)*TZ; mH=LB*4*exp(3*alpha/6)*TH
    spec5=(3*z5+5*pi**2*z3)/(8*pi**4); k5=exp(spec5/6)/(8*pi**3); L5=(2*pi/sqrt(3))*v*k5**3

def report(p,path):
    lines=["# THEA vs TOPOMAGIC — Controlled Audit v1.0.0","",f"Stable mathematical-payload SHA-256: `{p['stable_sha256']}`","","## Score ledger","",f"- Checks: **{p['check_count']}**"]
    for k in ("PASS","CONDITIONAL","OPEN","CORRECTION","REFUTED"): lines.append(f"- {k}: **{p['status_counts'].get(k,0)}**")
    lines += ["","A PASS is narrow: it does not transfer theorem status to adjacent interpretations.",""]
    group=None
    for c in p["checks"]:
        if c["group"]!=group: group=c["group"]; lines += [f"## {group.title()}",""]
        lines += [f"### {c['id']} — {c['name']}","",f"**Status:** {c['status']}  ",f"**Mode:** {c['mode']}  ",f"**Result:** `{json.dumps(c['result'],ensure_ascii=False,sort_keys=True)}`","",c["evidence"],""]
    path.write_text("\n".join(lines),encoding="utf-8")

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument("--root",type=Path,default=Path(__file__).resolve().parent); ap.add_argument("--json",type=Path); ap.add_argument("--report",type=Path); a=ap.parse_args(); root=a.root.resolve()
    p=build(root); j=a.json or root/"topomagic_control_audit_receipt.json"; r=a.report or root/"topomagic_control_audit_report.md"
    j.write_text(json.dumps(p,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n",encoding="utf-8"); report(p,r)
    print("THEA vs TOPOMAGIC CONTROL AUDIT v"+VERSION); print("checks:",p["check_count"])
    for k,v in p["status_counts"].items(): print(f"{k:12s} {v}")
    print("stable sha256:",p["stable_sha256"]); print("receipt:",j); print("report:",r)
if __name__=="__main__": main()
```
